251:         if not m:

255:     def _core(self) -> str:
256:         """The subcircuit with any .lib line stripped — we add our own."""

266:             if isinstance(val, str):                 # a full source expression
267:                 lines.append(f"Vsrc{i} {node} 0 {val}")
268:             else:
269:                 lines.append(f"Vsrc{i} {node} 0 {val}")
270:         for node, spec in self.loads.items():
271:             lines.append(f"C_load_{node} {node} 0 {spec}")
272:         return lines
273: 
274:     def build_deck(self, analysis_lines: Sequence[str], control: Sequence[str]) -> str:
275:         """Assemble a complete, runnable ngspice deck."""
276:         if not os.path.isfile(self.pdk_models):
277:             raise FileNotFoundError(
278:                 f"PDK models not found at {self.pdk_models} — set PDK_ROOT/PDKPATH")
279:         head = []
280:         if os.path.isfile(self.pdk_design):
281:             # must come before the model library or the models fail to evaluate
282:             head.append(f".include '{self.pdk_design}'")
283:         head.append(f".lib '{self.pdk_models}' {self.corner}")
284:         if self.mismatch:
285:             head.append(".param sw_stat_mismatch=1")
286: 
287:         inst = f"Xdut {' '.join(self.ports)} {self.cell}"
288:         body = head + ["", self._core(), ""] + self._stimulus() + [inst, ""]
289:         body += list(analysis_lines)
290:         pre = [f"set seed={self.seed}"] if self.seed is not None else []
291:         body += [".control"] + pre + list(control) + [".endc", ".end", ""]
292:         return "\n".join(body)

303:         _parse_print(res, probes)
304:         paths = list(r.get("dat_paths") or [])
305:         # Read the file THIS analysis wrote. Testbenches reuse one workdir, so
306:         # ac.dat/dc.dat/tran.dat pile up side by side and taking the first
307:         # path silently returned a previous analysis's data — the transient
308:         # came back holding the DC sweep, with the output node missing.
309:         if datfile:
310:             want = [p for p in paths if os.path.basename(p) == datfile]
311:             paths = want or ([] if any(

313:         for path in paths:
314:             _parse_wrdata(res, path, x_name, probes, complex_data)
315:             break
316:         return res
317: 
318:     # -- analyses --
319:     def op(self) -> SimResult:
320:         """Operating point: one value per probe."""
321:         probes = self.probes or self.ports
322:         ctrl = ["op"] + [f"print v({p})" for p in probes]
323:         return self._run("op", self.build_deck([], ctrl), "", probes)
324: 
325:     def dc(self, source_node: str, start: float, stop: float, step: float) -> SimResult:
326:         """Sweep a source and record the probes."""
327:         idx = list(self.sources).index(source_node) if source_node in self.sources else None
328:         vname = f"Vsrc{idx}" if idx is not None else source_node
329:         # Default to every port, not just the swept source: recording only
330:         # the stimulus tells you what you already know and leaves the
331:         # response — the reason for the sweep — out of the result.
332:         probes = self.probes or self.ports or [source_node]
333:         ctrl = [f"dc {vname} {start} {stop} {step}",
334:                 f"wrdata dc.dat {' '.join('v(%s)' % p for p in probes)}"]
335:         return self._run("dc", self.build_deck([], ctrl), "v_sweep", probes,
336:                          datfile="dc.dat")
337: 
338:     def ac(self, fstart: float, fstop: float, points: int = 20,
339:            variation: str = "dec", ac_node: Optional[str] = None) -> SimResult:
340:         """AC sweep. The stimulus node is given an AC magnitude of 1."""
341:         node = ac_node or (list(self.sources)[0] if self.sources else None)
342:         if node is None:
343:             raise ValueError("ac() needs a source node to excite")
344:         probes = self.probes or self.ports or [node]
345:         deck = self.build_deck([], [f"ac {variation} {points} {fstart} {fstop}",
346:                                     f"wrdata ac.dat {' '.join('v(%s)' % p for p in probes)}"])
347:         # Give the chosen source an AC magnitude of 1. The old pattern only
348:         # matched a single-token value, so a source written "DC 1.2" kept an
349:         # AC magnitude of zero and the sweep came back all zeros — a silent
350:         # wrong answer. Match the line, not the value, and refuse to run if
351:         # the source is not there at all.
352:         idx = list(self.sources).index(node)
353:         deck, n = re.subn(rf"^(Vsrc{idx}\s+{re.escape(node)}\s+0\s+.*)$",
354:                           r"\1 AC 1", deck, flags=re.MULTILINE)
355:         if n != 1:
356:             raise RuntimeError(
357:                 f"ac(): could not attach an AC magnitude to source "
358:                 f"'Vsrc{idx}' on node '{node}'. Without it every result is "
359:                 f"zero, so this fails instead of returning one.")
360:         return self._run("ac", deck, "frequency", probes, datfile="ac.dat",
361:                          complex_data=True)
362:

368:         return self._run("tran", self.build_deck([], ctrl), "time", probes,

371:     def monte_carlo(self, analysis: str = "op", runs: int = 25,
372:                     corner: str = "statistical", mismatch: bool = True,
373:                     **kwargs) -> MonteCarloResult:
374:         """Repeat an analysis with fresh device-mismatch draws.
375: 
376:         GF180 expresses mismatch as `delvto = mis_vth * sw_stat_mismatch`, so
377:         two things are needed for the samples to actually differ: a corner that
378:         carries the statistical parameters, and sw_stat_mismatch enabled. Just
379:         re-seeding the typical corner returns the same number every run — the
380:         mismatch term is multiplied by zero.
381:         """
382:         out = MonteCarloResult(measure=analysis)
383:         fn = {"op": self.op, "dc": self.dc, "ac": self.ac, "tran": self.tran}[analysis]
384:         base_wd, base_corner, base_mm, base_seed = (
385:             self.workdir, self.corner, self.mismatch, self.seed)
386:         self.corner, self.mismatch = corner, mismatch
387:         try:
388:             for i in range(runs):
389:                 self.workdir = os.path.join(base_wd, f"mc{i:03d}")
390:                 self.seed = i + 1
391:                 out.runs.append(fn(**kwargs))
392:         finally:
393:             self.workdir, self.corner = base_wd, base_corner
394:             self.mismatch, self.seed = base_mm, base_seed
395:         return out

Now I have a good understanding. Let me plan the approach.

The plan:
1. Write a driver script that:
   - Defines the OTA netlist. The user wants simplest OTA topology that can satisfy specs. A 5T OTA (NMOS diff pair, PMOS current mirror load, NMOS tail) with external IBIAS. But 5T OTA with CL=5pF and GBW>=1MHz, gain>=35dB, slew>=0.5V/us, PM>=60°, supply current <= 250uA.

   Actually, with the 5T OTA: gm1 = sqrt(2*Kp*(W/L)*I_tail/2). Let's estimate. IBIAS=20uA externally. Hmm. IBIAS is an external bias current input, so the tail current would be a mirror of IBIAS. Let's design a 5T OTA with NMOS tail mirroring IBIAS.

   Standard self-biased: M7 gate tied to the **diode-connected M3** node? For NMOS-input first stage, netA = VDD - |Vgs3| ≈ 2.55V. M7 PMOS gate at Vbias where Vbias = netA = 2.55V → Vgs7 = 2.55-3.3 = -0.75 → Vov7 = 0.1, current small. And M6 gate = netA = 2.55V → VGS6 = 2.55, M6 wants huge current → Vout → VSS. Ugh, still.

   The issue: when first stage output is used to drive both M6 and M7 gates, they fight. 

   The classic solution (Allen-Holberg two-stage): M6 (NMOS) gate = first-stage output (netA, ≈2.5V for NMOS input or 0.9V for PMOS input). M7 (PMOS) gate = the bias voltage Vbias from the bias circuit (≈ VDD - |Vgs| of a PMOS diode ≈ 2.5V or gate of M5-equivalent). Then:
   - PMOS-input first stage: netA≈0.9V. M6 NMOS gate 0.9V → VGS6 = 0.9 → Vov6=0.25. M7 PMOS gate=Vbias≈2.5-3.3... if Vbias = VDD - |Vtp| - Vov = 3.3-0.75-0.15 = 2.4V → Vgs7=-0.9 → Vov7=0.15.
   - I(M7) = 0.5Kp(W/L)7 Vov7². I(M6) = 0.5Kn(W/L)6 Vov6².
   - Balance: choose (W/L)6 and (W/L)7 so both saturate with equal current at Vout=VDD/2. As discussed, once I6=I7 in saturation, Vout is free (second-order). But if we SIZE them so I6=I7 exactly, then M6 and M7 both want the same current regardless of Vout (in saturation), so Vout sits wherever — determined by the gain*offset of the first stage actually! Because netA is set by the first stage's differential balance: the first stage has its OWN feedback: M1/M2 diff pair + M3/M4 mirror. netA is defined as VDD - |VGS3|. So netA is well-defined. And Vout is set by M6/M7 balance which IS a well-defined KCL equation: I(M6, VGS6=netA, VDS6=Vout) = I(M7, VGS7=Vbias, VSD7=VDD-Vout). In first order (no CLM), this is an equation that determines Vout only via which device enters triode. With second-order CLM, Vout is determined smoothly somewhere in the middle. In real designs it lands mid-rail with modest sensitivity. This actually works and is the standard textbook two-stage. The output DC will be approximately where the two saturation currents cross, which with matched-ish sizing is near mid-rail. Good enough, and it can be tuned.

   But honestly the simpler and more deterministic approach for mid-rail DC: use **a PMOS diff pair + NMOS diode-connected load, output at the drain of the NMOS mirror (≈0.8V), then the output of the whole OTA is this node** — NO. fails.

```python
def _make_deck(tb, extra_currents):
    deck = tb.build_deck([], [])
    # insert current sources before .control
    lines = deck.splitlines()
    out = []
    inserted = False
    for line in lines:
        if line.strip().lower().startswith(".control") and not inserted:
            for node, val in extra_currents.items():
                out.append(f"Ibias_{node} {node} 0 DC {val}")
            inserted = True
        out.append(line)
    return "\n".join(out)
```

168: def fft(result: SimResult, signal: str, *, window: str = "hann",
169:         drop_dc: bool = True) -> Tuple[List[float], List[float]]:
170:     """Single-sided amplitude spectrum of a transient signal.
171: 
172:     Returns (frequencies_hz, magnitudes). The transient is resampled onto a
173:     uniform grid first, because ngspice uses adaptive time steps and an FFT of
174:     unevenly spaced samples is meaningless.
175:     """
176:     if result.analysis != "tran":
177:         raise ValueError(f"fft needs a transient result, got {result.analysis!r}")
178:     import numpy as np
179: 
180:     t = np.asarray(result.x, dtype=float)
181:     y = np.asarray(result.get(signal), dtype=float)
182:     if t.size < 4:
183:         raise ValueError("not enough transient points for an FFT")
184: 
185:     n = int(2 ** math.floor(math.log2(t.size)))          # power of two
186:     tu = np.linspace(t[0], t[-1], n)
187:     yu = np.interp(tu, t, y)
188:     dt = float(tu[1] - tu[0])
189: 
190:     if window == "hann":
191:         w = np.hanning(n)
192:     elif window in (None, "none", "rect"):
193:         w = np.ones(n)
194:     else:
195:         raise ValueError(f"unknown window {window!r}")
196:     coherent_gain = w.mean()
197: 
198:     spec = np.fft.rfft((yu - yu.mean()) * w)
199:     mag = np.abs(spec) / (n * coherent_gain) * 2.0
200:     freq = np.fft.rfftfreq(n, dt)
201:     if drop_dc:
202:         freq, mag = freq[1:], mag[1:]
203:     return freq.tolist(), mag.tolist()
204: 
205: 
206: # ── testbench ──────────────────────────────────────────────────────────

410: def _parse_wrdata(res: SimResult, path: str, x_name: str,
411:                   probes: Sequence[str], complex_data: bool = False) -> None:
412:     """Read an ngspice `wrdata` file.
413: 
414:     wrdata repeats the sweep variable before every probe, so the stride
415:     depends on whether the data is real or complex:
416: 
417:         real (dc/tran)  x y   x y   x y    -> stride 2
418:         complex (ac)    x re im  x re im   -> stride 3
419: 
420:     Parsing an AC file with the real stride silently produced phantom signals
421:     (`col4`, `col5`) and put an imaginary part where a voltage belonged, so
422:     the two cases are handled separately. For AC the magnitude is stored under
423:     the probe name, with the real and imaginary parts kept alongside as
424:     `<name>.re` / `<name>.im` for anyone who needs phase.
425:     """
426:     if not os.path.isfile(path):
427:         return
428:     xs: List[float] = []
429:     cols: List[List[float]] = []
430:     with open(path) as f:
431:         for line in f:
432:             line = line.strip()
433:             if not line or line[0] in "#*@":
434:                 continue
435:             try:
436:                 vals = [float(v) for v in line.split()]
437:             except ValueError:
438:                 continue
439:             if not vals:
440:                 continue
441:             xs.append(vals[0])
442:             if complex_data and len(vals) >= 3:
443:                 ys = [(vals[i], vals[i + 1])
444:                       for i in range(1, len(vals) - 1, 3)]
445:             elif len(vals) > 2:
446:                 ys = vals[1::2]
447:             else:
448:                 ys = vals[1:]
449:             while len(cols) < len(ys):
450:                 cols.append([])
451:             for i, v in enumerate(ys):
452:                 cols[i].append(v)
453:     if not xs:
454:         return
455:     res.x = xs
456:     res.x_name = x_name
457:     for i, col in enumerate(cols):
458:         name = probes[i] if i < len(probes) else f"col{i}"
459:         if complex_data:
460:             re_p = [c[0] for c in col]
461:             im_p = [c[1] for c in col]
462:             mag = [math.hypot(a, b) for a, b in col]
463:             res.signals[f"{name}.re"] = re_p
464:             res.signals[f"{name}.im"] = im_p
465:             col = mag
466:         res.signals[name] = col

```
def run_spice(netlist_content, *, workdir=None, timeout=300, fmt="raw"):
    """Run any ngspice netlist.

    Args:
        netlist_content: Full SPICE netlist as string.
        workdir: If None, uses temp dir (auto-cleaned).
        timeout: Max seconds.
        fmt: 'raw' for binary, 'dat' for wrdata text, or 'both'.

    Returns:
        {"stdout": str, "stderr": str, "returncode": int,
         "raw_path": str|None, "dat_paths": [str],
         "workdir": str}
    """
    wd = os.path.abspath(workdir) if workdir else tempfile.mkdtemp(prefix="ngspice_")
    os.makedirs(wd, exist_ok=True)
    # Absolute: the subprocess runs with cwd=wd, so a relative path here made
    # ngspice look for <wd>/<wd>/input.spice and fail with "No such file".
    inp = os.path.join(wd, "input.spice")
    with open(inp, "w") as f:
        f.write(netlist_content)

    try:
        r = subprocess.run(["ngspice", "-b", inp], capture_output=True, text=True,
                           timeout=timeout, cwd=wd)
    except subprocess.TimeoutExpired:
        print(f"[TIMEOUT] ngspice timed out after {timeout}s — workdir kept: {wd}")
        return {"stdout": "", "stderr": "", "returncode": -1,
                "raw_path": None, "dat_paths": [], "workdir": wd}

    # Find output files
    raw_path = None
    dat_paths = []
    for f in os.listdir(wd):
        if f.endswith(".raw"):
            raw_path = os.path.join(wd, f)
        elif f.endswith(".dat"):
            dat_paths.append(os.path.join(wd, f))

    if r.returncode != 0:
        print(f"[WARN] ngspice returned {r.returncode} — workdir kept: {wd}")

    return {
        "stdout": r.stdout,
        "stderr": r.stderr,
        "returncode": r.returncode,
        "raw_path": raw_path,
        "dat_paths": dat_paths,
        "workdir": wd,
    }


# ── Raw file to CSV ──────────────────────────────────────
def raw_to_csv(raw_path, csv_path=None, max_rows=None):

```

```python
class OtaTB(Testbench):
    def __init__(self, *a, extra_currents=None, corner="typical", temp=27, **k):
        super().__init__(*a, **k)
        self.extra_currents = dict(extra_currents or {})
        self.temp = temp
    def _stimulus(self):
        lines = super()._stimulus()
        for node, val in self.extra_currents.items():
            lines.append(f"Ibias_{node} {node} 0 DC {val}")
        return lines
```

```python
def build_deck(self, analysis_lines, control):
    deck = super().build_deck(analysis_lines, control)
    # insert .temp after the .lib line
    lines = deck.splitlines()
    out = []
    inserted = False
    for ln in lines:
        out.append(ln)
        if ln.strip().startswith(".lib") and not inserted:
            out.append(f".temp {self.temp}")
            inserted = True
    return "\n".join(out)
```

```python
def measure_all(netlist, cell, *, out_node="OUT", in_node="INP", vdd=3.3, vss=0.0,
                vcm=1.65, ibias=20e-6, cl=5e-12, corner="typical", temp=27,
                workdir=None):
    supplies = {"VDD": vdd, "VSS": vss}
    sources = {"INP": f"DC {vcm}", "INN": f"DC {vcm}"}
    loads = {out_node: f"{cl:f}"}   # cl in farads
    tb = OtaTB(netlist, cell, supplies=supplies, sources=sources, loads=loads,
               corner=corner, temp=temp, extra_currents={"IBIAS": ibias},
               probes=[out_node], workdir=workdir or "sim", timeout=600)
    m = {}

    # --- AC: gain, bw, ugf, phase margin ---
    ac = tb.ac(1e1, 1e9, points=30, ac_node=in_node)
    if not ac.ok or not ac.x:
        return {}
    try:
        mag = ac.get(out_node)
    except KeyError:
        return {}
    if not mag or max(mag) <= 0:
        return {}
    db = [20*log10(abs(v)) for v in mag]
    gain_db = db[0]
    m["gain_db"] = gain_db
    # bw -3dB
    edge = gain_db - 3
    bw = None
    for f, d in zip(ac.x, db):
        if d <= edge:
            bw = f; break
    m["bw_hz"] = bw if bw is not None else float("nan")
    # ugf
    ugf = None
    for f, d in zip(ac.x, db):
        if d <= 0:
            ugf = f; break
    m["ugf_hz"] = ugf if ugf is not None else float("nan")
    # phase margin: phase at ugf
    re = ac.signals.get(f"{out_node}.re"); im = ac.signals.get(f"{out_node}.im")
    pm = float("nan")
    if ugf is not None and re and im:
        for f, r, i in zip(ac.x, re, im):
            if f >= ugf:
                ph = math.degrees(math.atan2(i, r))
                pm = 180 + ph
                break
    m["pm_deg"] = pm
    ...
```

Hmm wait, phase margin = 180 + phase(ugf). For a two-pole system, phase at ugf = -90°(dominant) - atan(ugf/p2). PM = 180 - 90 - atan(...) = 90 - atan(ugf/p2). If p2 at 175MHz, atan(3.2/175)=1° → PM=89°. Good.

Actually the sign: out_node OUT is the drain of M2; the AC response from INP. For 5T NMOS-input, the gain is inverting? INP→M1 gate→net1(drain M1)→M4 mirror→OUT. Wait: the output is at drain of M2. M2 gate = INN. M1 gate = INP. Hmm. The signal path: INP (M1 gate) → M1 drain (net1) → M3 diode → net1 voltage follows... The output at M2 drain. The differential gain is (Vout)/(Vinp - Vinn). Standard 5T: Vout = -gm2 * Rout * (Vinp - Vinn)? Let me think: Actually in the standard 5T, the output is at M2's drain, and M2's gate is INN. Hmm wait in the example netlist:
```
XM1 net1 inp net2 vss   ; M1: gate=inp, drain=net1
XM2 out  inm net2 vss   ; M2: gate=inm, drain=out
```
So OUT is at M2's drain, driven by INN. And net1 is M1's drain, drives M4's gate (mirror). The signal flow: a rise in INP (M1 gate) → M1 conducts more → net1 drops → M4 (mirror) gate drops → M4 conducts more → pulls OUT down. And a rise in INN → M2 conducts more → pulls OUT down directly. So both positive input rises pull OUT down. Vout = -gm * Rout * (Vinp + Vinn)?? No wait.

Let me redo: For differential: Vout = -gm1*Rout*(Vinp) - gm2*Rout*(Vinn) + [mirror contribution]. Actually:
- M2 (gate=INN): directly pulls OUT. d(Vout)/d(INN) = -gm2*Rout.
- M1 (gate=INP): d(net1)/d(INP) = -gm1*(Rout1) where Rout1 = ro1||ro3(diode≈1/gm3) ≈ 1/gm3. So d(net1)/d(INP) ≈ -gm1/gm3. M4 mirror: d(I_M4) = gm4 * d(net1)/d(INP)... M4 is a current source (mirror), its current = (W4/W3)*I_M3. d(I_M4)/d(INP) = (W4/W3)*d(I_M3)/d(INP). I_M3 = M3 current = gm3*(VDD - net1)... d(I_M3)/d(net1) = -gm3. So d(I_M4)/d(INP) = -(W4/W3)*gm3*(−gm1/gm3) = (W4/W3)*gm1. Current INTO OUT node from M4 increases by gm1 (for W4=W3). This current flows into OUT, raising Vout: d(Vout)/d(INP) = +gm1*Rout.

```python
def _run(tb, *, control, datfile=None, x_name="", probes=(), complex_data=False):
    deck = tb.build_deck([], control)
    r = run_spice(deck, workdir=tb.workdir, timeout=tb.timeout, fmt="dat")
    res = SimResult(analysis=..., x_name=..., returncode=r["returncode"], stdout=r.get("stdout",""), stderr=r.get("stderr",""), workdir=r.get("workdir",""), deck=deck)
    _parse_print(res, probes)
    if datfile:
        for p in r.get("dat_paths") or []:
            if os.path.basename(p) == datfile:
                _parse_wrdata(res, p, x_name, probes, complex_data)
                break
    return res
```

def _stimulus(self):
    lines = []
    i = 0
    for node, val in self.supplies.items():
        v = f"{val}" 
        if node in self.ac_nodes:
            v += " AC 1"
        lines.append(f"Vsupply{i} {node} 0 {v}")
        self._supply_name = {node: f"Vsupply{i}"}
        i += 1
    for node, val in self.sources.items():
        v = str(val)
        if node in self.ac_nodes:
            v += " AC 1"
        lines.append(f"Vsrc{len([x for x in self.sources]) + 0} ...")
```

Let me fully override `_stimulus`:

```python
def _stimulus(self):
    lines = []
    for i, (node, val) in enumerate(self.supplies.items()):
        v = str(val)
        if node in self.ac_nodes:
            v = v.rstrip() + " AC 1"
        lines.append(f"Vsupply{i} {node} 0 {v}")
        self.supply_srcs[node] = f"Vsupply{i}"
    for i, (node, val) in enumerate(self.sources.items()):
        v = str(val)
        if node in self.ac_nodes:
            v = v.rstrip() + " AC 1"
        lines.append(f"Vsrc{i} {node} 0 {v}")
        self.src_srcs[node] = f"Vsrc{i}"
    for node, val in self.loads.items():
        lines.append(f"C_load_{node} {node} 0 {val}")
    for node, val in self.extra_currents.items():
        lines.append(f"Ibias_{node} {node} 0 DC {val}")
    return lines
```

This overrides the base cleanly and I track supply/source names. The base methods op/ac/dc/tran use these names via list(self.sources).index(node) etc. — compatible since I keep Vsrc{i} naming by sources order. The supply name for op current: `Vsupply0` for VDD (first supply). Good — but note base used `Vsupply%d` too. 

For the current source in `extra_currents`: value could be "20u". I'll pass as string.

```python
def custom(self, analysis, control, *, datfile, x_name, probes, complex_data=False):
    deck = self.build_deck([], control)
    r = run_spice(deck, workdir=self.workdir, timeout=self.timeout, fmt="dat")
    res = SimResult(...)
    _parse_print(res, probes)
    for p in r.get("dat_paths") or []:
        if os.path.basename(p) == datfile:
            _parse_wrdata(res, p, x_name, probes, complex_data)
            break
    return res
```

Let me define `parse_ports`:
```python
def parse_ports(netlist, cell):
    m = re.search(rf"^\.subckt\s+{re.escape(cell)}\s+(.+)$", netlist, re.M | re.I)
    if not m:
        raise ValueError(f".subckt {cell} not found")
    ports = m.group(1).split()
    roles = {"vdd": None, "vss": None, "inp": None, "inn": None, "out": None, "ibias": None}
    for p in ports:
        pl = p.lower()
        if pl in ("vdd", "vcc") or "vdd" == pl or pl.startswith("vd"):
            if roles["vdd"] is None: roles["vdd"] = p
        elif pl in ("vss", "gnd", "vss!") or pl.startswith("vs"):
            if roles["vss"] is None: roles["vss"] = p
        elif "ibias" in pl or "bias" in pl or pl in ("ib", "vb"):
            if roles["ibias"] is None: roles["ibias"] = p
        elif pl in ("out", "o", "vo") or pl.startswith("out"):
            if roles["out"] is None: roles["out"] = p
        elif pl in ("inp", "in", "ip", "vip", "vinp", "vin", "p", "inp"):
            if roles["inp"] is None: roles["inp"] = p
        elif pl in ("inn", "inm", "im", "vinm", "vinn", "in", "n"):
            if roles["inn"] is None: roles["inn"] = p
    missing = [r for r, v in roles.items() if v is None]
    if missing:
        raise ValueError(f"could not identify ports {missing} in {ports}")
    return roles
```

Hmm careful with "vs" prefix matching: port "vss" matches "vs" start; but "VDD" doesn't. Also "in" matches both inp and inn patterns — order matters. Let me match more specific first: check exact names, then startswith.

Let me refine:
```python
def parse_ports(netlist, cell):
    m = re.search(...)
    ports = m.group(1).split()
    roles = {"vdd":None,"vss":None,"inp":None,"inn":None,"out":None,"ibias":None}
    for p in ports:
        pl = p.lower()
        if pl in ("vdd","vcc","avdd"): roles["vdd"] = roles["vdd"] or p
        elif pl in ("vss","gnd","avss","vss!"): roles["vss"] = roles["vss"] or p
        elif "ibias" in pl or "bias" in pl or pl in ("vb","ib"): roles["ibias"] = roles["ibias"] or p
        elif pl in ("out","vo","o","output"): roles["out"] = roles["out"] or p
        elif pl in ("inp","ip","vinp","vip","vin","inp!"): roles["inp"] = roles["inp"] or p
        elif pl in ("inn","inm","im","vinn","vinm","inm!"): roles["inn"] = roles["inn"] or p
        elif pl.startswith("in"):
            if "n" in pl and "p" not in pl: roles["inn"] = roles["inn"] or p
            else: roles["inp"] = roles["inp"] or p
        elif pl.startswith("vd"): roles["vdd"] = roles["vdd"] or p
        elif pl.startswith("vs"): roles["vss"] = roles["vss"] or p
    ...
```

    base = dict(supplies); base.update(sources)

Wait, my _stimulus writes `Vsrc{i} {node} 0 {val}` where val = "{vcm}". Then `.dc vcm 0.4 3.0 0.05` sweeps the param vcm. ngspice `.dc` supports param sweep. Good. Let me define ICMR = largest contiguous vcm range where v(OUT) stays within [0.5, 2.8]. I'll compute from icmr.dat.

Hmm — but is that a good ICMR? When VCM is too low (below ~1V), the diff pair cuts off, tail current drops, output → VDD? Let's see: at low VCM, M1/M2 off, M5 tail in triode (gate at VCM... tail device M5 gate at IBIAS, not VCM). Actually M5 gate is at IBIAS (fixed), so tail current stays, but M1/M2 can't pass it (their VGS low). Then the mirror M3/M4: M3 diode still conducts the... hmm, M3 needs current. If M1 off, no current through M3, M3 in triode, net1 → VDD, M4 off, OUT floats (driven by M2 off and M4 off) → OUT undefined/high-impedance. Output may rail. So ICMR low bound ~ Vgs1 + Vds5 = ~0.9V. High bound: VCM high → M1/M2 deep on, mirror M3/M4 still saturated until VCM approaches VDD - |Vtp| - ... The DC sweep will reveal it.

Actually, at high VCM (e.g., 2.8), M1/M2 gate high, their source (net2) rises, tail M5 goes into triode, tail current drops → output changes. The output DC will start moving. So the "output stays in [0.5,2.8]" region gives a reasonable ICMR.

Alternatively, a cleaner ICMR: where the differential gain is within 3dB of peak. But that requires per-VCM gain measurement (many sims). The DC-transfer approach is a reasonable characterization. I'll report icmr as that range and note the definition.

control = ["tran 10n 60u", "wrdata tran.dat v(out)"]
```
For slew: step 1.45→1.85 (0.4V). Output slews. Compute max positive slope (rise) and max negative slope (fall) of v(OUT). Then for swing, do a bigger pulse: PULSE(0.5 2.8 ...) and take min/max of v(OUT).

Two transients. Or combine: big pulse for swing, and compute slew on the same (slew rate is I_tail/CL independent of step size). Let me use one transient with PULSE(0.6 2.7 0 20n 20n 30u 60u) and measure both swing (min/max of v(out)) and slew (max/min slopes). The slew rate measured on a 2V step is the max slope — fine.

Actually, be careful: with a 2V step, the input stage saturates quickly and output rails — slew measured correctly. Good.

Slew measurement: numerically differentiate v(OUT) vs t. Use central differences on the parsed transient. find max derivative (V/s) and min derivative (V/s, most negative). slew_rise_vus = max_slope/1e6; slew_fall_vus = abs(min_slope)/1e6.

```python
hooks = make_hooks(cell=CELL, in_node="INP", out_node="OUT",
                   supplies={"VDD": 3.3, "VSS": 0.0},
                   bias={"INP": "DC 1.65", "INN": "DC 1.65", "IBIAS": "DC 0"},  # IBIAS unused by base
                   spec_names=[s.name for s in SPECS],
                   specs=SPECS,
                   outdir=OUTDIR, verbosity=1)

def _sim_pre(design):
    m = measure_all(design.netlist, CELL, workdir=os.path.join(OUTDIR, "sim", "pre"))
    if not m:
        raise RuntimeError("pre-layout simulation produced no data")
    return m

def _sim_pex(design, layout):
    path = layout.pex_netlist
    with open(path) as f: pex_netlist = f.read()
    m = measure_all(pex_netlist, CELL, workdir=os.path.join(OUTDIR, "sim", "pex"))
    if not m:
        raise RuntimeError("PEX simulation produced no data")
    return m

hooks.simulate_pre = _sim_pre
hooks.simulate_pex = _sim_pex
```

def default_strategy() -> SearchStrategy:
    return CompositeStrategy()


# ── Pareto archive ────────────────────────────────────────────────────

@dataclass
class ParetoArchive:
    """A small set of non-dominated designs, for later trade-off decisions."""
    entries: List[Dict[str, object]] = field(default_factory=list)
    limit: int = 8

    def offer(self, result: CandidateResult) -> None:
        if not result.ok or result.report is None:
            return
        m = dict(result.metrics)
        entry = {"id": result.candidate.id, "score": result.score,
                 "margin_pct": result.margin, "metrics": m,
                 "change": result.candidate.change}
        for e in self.entries:
            if self._dominates(e["metrics"], m) and e["score"] <= result.score:
                return
        self.entries = [e for e in self.entries
                        if not (self._dominates(m, e["metrics"])
                                and result.score <= e["score"])]
        self.entries.append(entry)
        self.entries.sort(key=lambda e: e["score"])
        del self.entries[self.limit:]

    @staticmethod
    def _dominates(a: Mapping[str, float], b: Mapping[str, float]) -> bool:
        keys = set(a) & set(b)
        if not keys:
            return False
        return all(a[k] >= b[k] for k in keys) and any(a[k] > b[k] for k in keys)

    def as_list(self) -> List[Dict[str, object]]:
        return list(self.entries)

```

319: class LineSearchStrategy(SearchStrategy):
320:     """Continue along a direction that already produced an improvement.
321: 
322:     This is the move the old optimizer was missing. Once a step helps, the
323:     next iteration extends it *and* brackets it, so the search can overshoot

325:     """
326:     name = "line_search"
327: 
328:     def propose(self, state: SearchState, budget: int) -> List[Candidate]:
329:         accepted = [t for t in state.memory.trials

331:         if not accepted:
332:             return []
333:         last = accepted[-1]
334:         f = float(last["params"].get("factor", 1.0))
335:         if f == 1.0:
336:             return []
337:         # Extend, repeat, and half-step: bracket the optimum instead of
338:         # assuming the trend continues. The measured sweep is non-monotonic,
339:         # so extrapolation alone would be wrong.
340:         step_sizes = [f * f, f, 1.0 - (1.0 - f) / 2.0] if f < 1 else [f * f, f,
341:                                                                      1.0 + (f - 1.0) / 2.0]

roles[\"vss\"] or p\n    missing = [r for r, v in roles.items() if v is None]\n    if missing:\n        raise ValueError(f\"could not identify port roles {missing} \"\n                         f\"among {ports}\")\n    return roles\n\n\ndef _phase(res, name, f):\n    re_v = res.signals.get(f\"{name}.re\")\n    im_v = res.signals.get(f\"{name}.im\")\n    if not re_v or not im_v:\n        return None\n    for fr, rv, iv in zip(res.x, re_v, im_v):\n        if fr >= f:\n            return math.degrees(math.atan2(iv, rv))\n    return math.degrees(math.atan2(im_v[-1], re_v[-1]))\n\n\ndef _largest_contiguous(x, y, lo, hi):\n    best = None\n    cur = None\n    for xi, yi in zip(x, y):\n        if lo <= yi <= hi:\n            if cur is None:\n                cur = [xi, xi]\n            else:\n                cur[1] = xi\n        else:\n            if cur is not None:\n                if best is None or (cur[1] - cur[0]) > (best[1] - best[0]):\n                    best = cur\n                cur = None\n    if cur is not None and (best is None or (cur[1] - cur[0]) > (best[1] - best[0])):\n        best = cur\n    return (best[0], best[1]) if best else (None, None)\n\n\ndef measure_all(netlist, cell, *, vdd=3.3, vss=0.0, vcm=1.65, ibias=20e-6,\n                cl=5e-12, corner=\"typical\", temp=27.0, workdir=\"sim\"):\n    \"\"\"Measure the full OTA metric set on any netlist (schematic or extracted).\n\n    Returns an empty dict on any core-measurement failure so the flow treats\n    it as a tool failure. Optional characterizations (CMRR/PSRR/ICMR/noise/\n    offset) degrade to ``None`` rather than failing the run.\n    \"\"\"\n    mcell = re.search(r\"^\\.subckt\\s+(\\S+)\", netlist, re.MULTILINE | re.IGNORECASE)\n    if mcell:\n        cell = mcell.group(1)\n    roles = parse_ports(netlist, cell)\n    supplies = {roles[\"vdd\"]: vdd, roles[\"vss\"]: vss}\n    sources = {roles[\"inp\"]: f\"DC {vcm}\", roles[\"inn\"]: f\"DC {vcm}\"}\n    loads = {roles[\"out\"]: f\"{cl:.6g}\"}\n    extras = {roles[\"ibias\"]: f\"{ibias:.6g}\"}\n    out = roles[\"out\"]\n    inp = roles[\"inp\"]\n    wd = workdir\n    os.makedirs(wd, exist_ok=True)\n\n    try:\n        # ── main run: op + ac + dc transfer + noise ────────────────────\n        tb = OtaTB(netlist, cell, supplies=supplies,\n                   sources={inp: f\"DC {vcm}\", roles[\"inn\"]: f\"DC {vcm}\"},\n                   loads=loads, corner=corner, temp=temp,\n                   extra_currents=extras, ac_nodes={inp},\n                   probes=[out], workdir=wd, timeout=600)\n        vdd_src = tb.supply_srcs[roles[\"vdd\"]]\n        inp_src = tb.src_srcs[inp]\n        ctrl = [\n            \"op\",\n            f\"print v({out}) i({vdd_src})\",\n            \"ac dec 25 1 1e9\",\n            f\"wrdata ac.dat v({out})\",\n            f\"dc {inp_src} 0.4 2.9 0.005\",\n            f\"wrdata dc.dat v({out})\",\n            \"noise v(\" + out + \") \" + inp_src + \" dec 10 1 100Meg\",\n            \"print inoise_total onoise_total\",\n        ]\n        res = tb.custom(\"main\", ctrl, datfile=\"dc.dat\", x_name=\"inp\",\n                        probes=[out])\n        if not res.ok:\n            return {}\n\n        ac_res = tb.custom(\"ac\", [\"ac dec 25 1 1e9\", f\"wrdata ac2.dat v({out})\"],\n                           datfile=\"ac2.dat\", x_name=\"freq\", probes=[out],\n                           complex_data=True)\n        if not ac_res.ok or not ac_res.x:\n            return {}\n        mag = ac_res.get(out)\n        if not mag or max(mag) <= 0:\n            return {}\n        db = [20.0 * math.log10(v) for v in mag]\n        gain_db = db[0]\n        bw_hz = None\n        for f, d in zip(ac_res.x, db):\n            if d <= gain_db - 3.0:\n                bw_hz = f\n                break\n        ugf_hz = None\n        for f, d in zip(ac_res.x, db):\n            if d <= 0.0:\n                ugf_hz = f\n                break\n        pm_deg = None\n        if ugf_hz is not None:\n            ph = _phase(ac_res, out, ugf_hz)\n            if ph is not None:\n                pm_deg = 180.0 + ph\n\n        m = {\n            \"gain_db\": gain_db,\n            \"bw_hz\": bw_hz if bw_hz is not None else float(\"nan\"),\n            \"ugf_hz\": ugf_hz if ugf_hz is not None else float(\"nan\"),\n            \"pm_deg\": pm_deg if pm_deg is not None else float(\"nan\"),\n        }\n\n        # operating point\n        vout_dc = res.value(out)\n        idd = res.value(f\"i({vdd_src})\")\n        m[\"vout_dc\"] = vout_dc\n        m[\"idd_ua\"] = idd * 1e6\n\n        # DC transfer: swing + systematic offset\n        voutc = res.get(out)\n        m[\"vout_hi\"] = max(voutc)\n        m[\"vout_lo\"] = min(voutc)\n        off = None\n        for i in range(1, len(voutc)):\n            a, b = voutc[i - 1], voutc[i]\n            if (a - 1.65) * (b - 1.65) <= 0 and b != a:\n                vin = res.x[i - 1] + (1.65 - a) / (b - a) * (res.x[i] - res.x[i - 1])\n                off = (vin - vcm) * 1000.0\n                break\n        m[\"offset_mv\"] = off\n\n        # noise totals (input-/output-referred, integrated 1 Hz .. 100 MHz)\n        m[\"inoise_rms\"] = res.value(\"inoise_total\")\n        m[\"onoise_rms\"] = res.value(\"onoise_total\")\n    except Exception as e:                      # noqa: BLE001\n        print(f\"[MEASURE] core measurement failed: {type(e).__name__}: {e}\")\n        return {}\n\n    # ── common-mode AC (CMRR) ──────────────────────────────────────────\n    try:\n        tb_cm = OtaTB(netlist, cell, supplies=supplies,\n                      sources={inp: f\"DC {vcm}\", roles[\"inn\"]: f\"DC {vcm}\"},\n                      loads=loads, corner=corner, temp=temp,\n                      extra_currents=extras, ac_nodes={inp, roles[\"inn\"]},\n                      probes=[out], workdir=wd, timeout=600)\n        cm = tb_cm.custom(\"ac\", [\"ac dec 15 1 1e7\", f\"wrdata cm.dat v({out})\"],\n                          datfile=\"cm.dat\", x_name=\"freq\", probes=[out],\n                          complex_data=True)\n        if cm.ok and cm.get(out):\n            m[\"cmrr_db\"] = gain_db - 20.0 * math.log10(cm.get(out)[0])\n    except Exception:                           # noqa: BLE001\n        pass\n\n    # ── supply AC (PSRR) ───────────────────────────────────────────────\n    try:\n        tb_ps = OtaTB(netlist, cell,\n                      supplies={roles[\"vdd\"]: vdd, roles[\"vss\"]: vss},\n                      sources={inp: f\"DC {vcm}\", roles[\"inn\"]: f\"DC {vcm}\"},\n                      loads=loads, corner=corner, temp=temp,\n                      extra_currents=extras, ac_nodes={roles[\"vdd\"]},\n                      probes=[out], workdir=wd, timeout=600)\n        ps = tb_ps.custom(\"ac\", [\"ac dec 15 1 1e7\", f\"wrdata ps.dat v({out})\"],\n                          datfile=\"ps.dat\", x_name=\"freq\", probes=[out],\n                          complex_data=True)\n        if ps.ok and ps.get(out):\n            m[\"psrr_db\"] = gain_db - 20.0 * math.log10(ps.get(out)[0])\n    except Exception:                           # noqa: BLE001\n        pass\n\n    # ── input common-mode range ────────────────────────────────────────\n    try:\n        tb_ic = OtaTB(netlist, cell, supplies=supplies,\n                      sources={inp: \"{vcm}\", roles[\"inn\"]: \"{vcm}\"},\n                      loads=loads, corner=corner, temp=temp,\n                      extra_currents=extras, probes=[out], workdir=wd,\n                      timeout=600)\n        ic = tb_ic.custom(\"dc\", [\".param vcm=0.4\", \"dc vcm 0.4 3.0 0.05\",\n                                 f\"wrdata ic.dat v({out})\"],\n                          datfile=\"ic.dat\", x_name=\"vcm\", probes=[out],\n                          analysis_lines=[\".param vcm=0.4\"])\n        if ic.ok and ic.get(out):\n            lo, hi = _largest_contiguous(ic.x, ic.get(out), 0.5, 2.8)\n            if lo is not None:\n                m[\"icmr_lo\"] = lo\n                m[\"icmr_hi\"] = hi\n    except Exception:                           # noqa: BLE001\n        pass\n\n    # ── transient: slew rates + large-signal rails ─────────────────────\n    try:\n        tb_tr = OtaTB(netlist, cell, supplies=supplies,\n                      sources={inp: \"DC 0.6 PULSE(0.6 2.7 0 20n 20n 30u 60u)\",\n                               roles[\"inn\"]: f\"DC {vcm}\"},\n                      loads=loads, corner=corner, temp=temp,\n                      extra_currents=extras, probes=[out], workdir=wd,\n                      timeout=900)\n        tr = tb_tr.custom(\"tran\", [\"tran 10n 60u\", f\"wrdata tr.dat v({out})\"],\n                          datfile=\"tr.dat\", x_name=\"time\", probes=[out])\n        if tr.ok and tr.get(out):\n            yo = tr.get(out)\n            to = tr.x\n            slew = None\n            for i in range(2, len(to)):\n                dt = to[i] - to[i - 2]\n                if dt <= 0:\n                    continue\n                sl = (yo[i] - yo[i - 2]) / dt\n                slew = max(slew or sl, sl)\n            m[\"slew_rise_vus\"] = (slew / 1e6) if slew else float(\"nan\")\n            slev = None\n            for i in range(2, len(to)):\n                dt = to[i] - to[i - 2]\n                if dt <= 0:\n                    continue\n                sl = (yo[i] - yo[i - 2]) / dt\n                slev = min(slev if slev is not None else sl, sl)\n            m[\"slew_fall_vus\"] = (abs(slev) / 1e6) if slev else float(\"nan\")\n            m[\"tran_vout_hi\"] = max(yo)\n            m[\"tran_vout_lo\"] = min(yo)\n    except Exception:                           # noqa: BLE001\n        pass\n\n    return m\n\n\ndef _simulate_pre(design):\n    wd = os.path.join(OUTDIR, \"sim\", \"pre\")\n    m = measure_all(design.netlist, CELL, workdir=wd)\n    if not m:\n        raise RuntimeError(\"pre-layout simulation produced no usable data — \"\n                           \"check the ngspice log; this is a tool failure\")\n    return m\n\n\ndef _simulate_pex(design, layout):\n    if not layout.pex_netlist or not os.path.isfile(layout.pex_netlist):\n        raise RuntimeError(\"no extracted netlist to simulate\")\n    with open(layout.pex_netlist) as f:\n        pex_netlist = f.read()\n    wd = os.path.join(OUTDIR, \"sim\", \"pex\")\n    m = measure_all(pex_netlist, CELL, workdir=wd)\n    if not m:\n        raise RuntimeError(\"PEX simulation produced no usable data — \"\n                           \"the extracted netlist exists but did not \"\n                           \"simulate; this is a tool failure\")\n    return m\n\n\n# ── hooks ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────\nhooks = make_hooks(\n    cell=CELL, in_node=\"INP\", out_node=\"OUT\",\n    supplies={\"VDD\": 3.3, \"VSS\": 0.0},\n    spec_names=[s.name for s in ALL_SPECS],\n    specs=ALL_SPECS,\n    outdir=OUTDIR,\n    verbosity=1,\n)\nhooks.simulate_pre = _simulate_pre\nhooks.simulate_pex = _simulate_pex\n# Branch-and-compare must measure candidates with the SAME full metric set,\n# otherwise the promoted winner is chosen on gain/bw only.\nref = {\"build_layout\": hooks.build_layout, \"simulate_pex\": _simulate_pex}\nhooks.propose_candidates = make_candidate_proposer(\n    specs=ALL_SPECS, hooks_ref=ref, verbosity=1)\n\nconfig = FullAutoConfig.for_effort(\"normal\", outdir=OUTDIR)\n\n\n# ── entry point ───────────────────────────────────────────────────────\ndef main():\n    if \"--sanity\" in sys.argv:\n        m = measure_all(NETLIST, CELL, workdir=os.path.join(OUTDIR, \"sim\", \"sanity\"))\n        print(\"SANITY MEASUREMENT:\")\n        for k, v in sorted(m.items()):\n            print(f\"  {k:16s} = {v}\")\n        return 0\n\n    res = run_full_auto(REQUEST, hooks, cell=CELL, specs=ALL_SPECS,\n                        netlist=NETLIST, config=config)\n\n    print(\"\\n\" + \"=\" * 80)\n    if hasattr(res, \"summary\"):\n        print(res.summary())\n    print(\"STATUS:\", res.status)\n    print(\"TAPEOUT_READY:\", res.tapeout_ready)\n    print(\"Report:\", res.report_path)\n    return 0 if res.tapeout_ready else 1\n\n\nif __name__ == \"__main__\":\n    sys.exit(main())\n"
}

```python
def _slew_segment(to, yo, t_start, t_end):
    xs, ys = [], []
    for t, y in zip(to, yo):
        if t_start <= t <= t_end:
            xs.append(t); ys.append(y)
    if len(xs) < 4:
        return None
    lo, hi = min(ys), max(ys)
    span = hi - lo
    if span <= 1e-9:
        return None
    def cross(level):
        for i in range(1, len(xs)):
            a, b = ys[i-1], ys[i]
            if a < level <= b or a > level >= b:
                if b == a: return xs[i]
                return xs[i-1] + (level - a)/(b - a)*(xs[i] - xs[i-1])
        return None
    t10 = cross(lo + 0.1*span)
    t90 = cross(lo + 0.9*span)
    if t10 is None or t90 is None or t90 <= t10:
        return None
    return 0.8*span/(t90 - t10)
```

Then rise = _slew_segment(to, yo, 0, 20e-6), fall = _slew_segment(to, yo, 30e-6, 50e-6). Convert V/s → V/µs (/1e6).

## Assistant (Build · DeepSeek V4 Flash · 13.0s)

_Thinking:_

Now:
- gain 42.38 ✓
- ugf 4.37MHz ✓ (GBW)
- pm 90° ✓
- idd 43µA ✓

- swing [0, 3.3] ✓
- slew_rise 6.4 V/µs ✓
- slew_fall = NaN ✗ — the falling slew segment returned None. Why? The fall window [30µ, 50µ]. The fall crosses from high to low. Let me check: maybe the 10% crossing of the fall — the fall edge: output goes from 3.24 to 0.37 (tran_vout_hi=3.24, lo=0.37). But the DC swing is [0,3.3]. Hmm. In the fall segment, lo=0.37, hi=3.24. t10 = cross(lo+0.1*span) — the output starts at hi and falls, so first it crosses hi-0.1*span (which is 90% level, at the start) then hi-0.9*span. cross() finds the FIRST crossing of each level. For level (lo+0.1*span) = low level — the output starts high and goes down; the first crossing of the low level happens near the END of the fall. For level (lo+0.9*span) = high level — first crossing happens at the START of the fall (right after the rise, when output is at hi... wait the output is already at hi from the rise, then at t=30µ it starts falling). At t=30µ the output = 3.24 = hi. It crosses (lo+0.9*span) immediately. So t90_fall ≈ 30µ, t10_fall ≈ when it reaches lo+0.1*span. So t90 < t10 → returns None.

The issue: for a falling edge, the "10%" crossing occurs after the "90%" crossing. My cross() returns the first crossing of a level. For the fall: t(lo+0.9span) happens right at the start, t(lo+0.1span) at the end. So t10 = cross(low level) and t90 = cross(high level). I have them reversed. I compute t10 = cross(lo+0.1*span) → the crossing of the LOW level, which happens late. t90 = cross(lo+0.9*span) → crossing of HIGH level, happens early. So t90 < t10 → None.

Fix: for the fall, t_start_cross = cross(hi - 0.1*span) [the high 10% point] and t_end = cross(hi - 0.9*span) [the low 90% point]. General fix: within the segment, the edge goes from v_start to v_end. Compute crossings of (v_start_small offset) and (v_end). Actually simplest: for a segment, find the two crossing times for the two extreme levels (lo+0.1span and lo+0.9span) and take min and max of the two crossing times:
```python
t1 = cross(lo + 0.1*span)
t2 = cross(lo + 0.9*span)
if t1 is None or t2 is None: return None
tlo, thi = min(t1,t2), max(t1,t2)
if thi <= tlo: return None
return 0.8*span/(thi - tlo)
```
Since 0.8*span = (hi-lo) - 0.2*span = distance between the two levels. Good.

Let me fix _slew_segment.

Also note: the rising edge crosses both levels once each (first crossing of each on the rise). t1 (low) and t2 (high) both on rise: t1 < t2. Good. {
  "content": "import os, sys\nsys.path.insert(0, \"/home/huda/opensource-project/Microelectronic-Block-Generator/results/deepseek_ota\")\nos.environ.setdefault(\"PDKPATH\", os.path.expanduser(\"~/.volare/gf180mcuD\"))\nfrom run_ota import NETLIST, CELL, measure_all\n\nreq = [(\"gain_db\", 35, \">=\"), (\"ugf_hz\", 1e6, \">=\"), (\"pm_deg\", 60, \">=\"),\n       (\"slew_rise_vus\", 0.5, \">=\"), (\"slew_fall_vus\", 0.5, \">=\"),\n       (\"idd_ua\", 250, \"<=\"), (\"vout_dc\", (1.15, 2.15), \"in\"),\n       (\"vout_hi\", 2.8, \">=\"), (\"vout_lo\", 0.5, \"<=\")]\n\ncorners = [\"typical\", \"ff\", \"ss\"]\nfor corner in corners:\n    for temp in (-40, 27, 125):\n        for vdd in (3.0, 3.3, 3.6):\n            m = measure_all(NETLIST, CELL, vdd=vdd, temp=temp, corner=corner,\n                            workdir=f\"/tmp/pvt/{corner}_{temp}_{vdd}\")\n            if not m:\n                print(f\"{corner:8s} {temp:5d}C {vdd:.1f}V : FAILED\"); continue\n            fails = []\n            for name, target, op in req:\n                v = m.get(name)\n                if v is None or v != v:\n                    fails.append(f\"{name}=NA\")\n                elif op == \">=\" and v < target:\n                    fails.append(f\"{name}={v:.2g}<{target}\")\n                elif op == \"<=\" and v > target:\n                    fails.append(f\"{name}={v:.2g}>{target}\")\n                elif op == \"in\" and not (target[0] <= v <= target[1]):\n                    fails.append(f\"{name}={v:.2g} not in {target}\")\n            print(f\"{corner:8s} {temp:5d}C {vdd:.1f}V : gain={m['gain_db']:5.1f} \"\n                  f\"ugf={m['ugf_hz']/1e6:5.2f}M pm={m['pm_deg']:4.1f} \"\n                  f\"idd={m['idd_ua']:6.1f} vdc={m['vout_dc']:5.3f} \"\n                  f\"sr={m['slew_rise_vus']:.1f}/{m['slew_fall_vus']:.1f} \"\n                  + (\"FAIL: \" + \"; \".join(fails) if fails else \"PASS\"))\n",
  "filePath": "/tmp/ota_vars/test_pvt.py"
}

  "content": "import os, sys\nsys.path.insert(0, \"/home/huda/opensource-project/Microelectronic-Block-Generator/results/deepseek_ota\")\nos.environ.setdefault(\"PDKPATH\", os.path.expanduser(\"~/.volare/gf180mcuD\"))\nfrom run_ota import measure_all\n\nlib = os.path.expanduser(\"~/.volare/gf180mcuD/libs.tech/ngspice/sm141064.ngspice\")\n\ndef mk(w34):\n    return f'''\n.lib \"{lib}\" typical\n.subckt ota VDD VSS INP INN OUT IBIAS\nXM1  net1 INP net2 VSS nfet_03v3 L=1u W=4u nf=4\nXM2  OUT  INN net2 VSS nfet_03v3 L=1u W=4u nf=4\nXM3  net1 net1 VDD VDD pfet_03v3 L=1u W={w34}u nf=4\nXM4  OUT  net1 VDD VDD pfet_03v3 L=1u W={w34}u nf=4\nXM5  net2 IBIAS VSS VSS nfet_03v3 L=1u W=8u nf=4\nXM6  IBIAS IBIAS VSS VSS nfet_03v3 L=1u W=4u nf=4\n.ends\n'''.strip()\n\ngrid = [(\"ss\", 125, 3.0), (\"typical\", 27, 3.3), (\"ff\", -40, 3.6)]\nfor w34 in (2.0, 2.4, 2.8, 3.2):\n    res = {}\n    for corner, temp, vdd in grid:\n        m = measure_all(mk(w34), \"ota\", vdd=vdd, temp=temp, corner=corner,\n                        workdir=f\"/tmp/pvt2/{w34}_{corner}_{temp}_{vdd}\")\n        res[(corner, temp, vdd)] = m[\"vout_dc\"] if m else None\n    lo = min(v for v in res.values() if v)\n    hi = max(v for v in res.values() if v)\n    nom = res[(\"typical\", 27, 3.3)]\n    print(f\"W34={w34}u  nom={nom:.3f}  pvt range=[{lo:.3f},{hi:.3f}]  \"\n          f\"lo_ok={lo>=1.15}  hi_ok={hi<=2.15}  center={(lo+hi)/2:.3f}\")\n",

}

Let me estimate PEX iteration cost: layout gen ~40-60s, KLayout DRC ~13s, Magic DRC ~1s, LVS ~5s, PEX extraction ~10-20s, PEX sim (5 sims) ~10-15s. Total ~90-120s per candidate. 12 iterations × 3 candidates × ~100s = 3600s = 1 hour just for PEX. Plus the pre-layout. Total maybe 1-1.5 hours. 

That's long but acceptable for a full run. Let me launch it in the background and monitor.

Actually — wait, I should reconsider: the PEX loop only starts after pre-layout passes. Pre-layout should pass on iteration 0 (all specs met at nominal). So pre-loop will do 1-2 iterations and pass → proceed to layout. Then PEX loop: layout + DRC + LVS + PEX sim. If PEX specs pass on the first layout, it's done in 1 PEX iteration (3 candidates? No — candidates are only evaluated when specs FAIL. The flow's `_advance` with propose_candidates is called only when the PEX report fails. If PEX passes, it returns immediately. So on success, only 1 PEX iteration with the base design, no candidates).

Wait, let me re-check: in the flow, after the first PEX sim, if report.passed → status=PASS, return. So if the layout passes DRC/LVS/PEX and the extracted netlist meets specs, the whole thing is 1 PEX iteration (no branching). 

So expected runtime: pre-loop (1-2 iters) + 1 layout build + verification + PEX sim. Probably 5-10 minutes total. The 12/12/3 budget is just the cap.

27:_OPS = {
28:    ">=": lambda v, t, tol: v >= t,

30:    "<=": lambda v, t, tol: v <= t,
31:    "<":  lambda v, t, tol: v < t,
32:    "==": lambda v, t, tol: abs(v - t) <= (tol if tol is not None else 0.0),
33:    "~=": lambda v, t, tol: abs(v - t) <= abs(t) * (tol if tol is not None else 0.1),
41:    ``tol`` applies only to ``==`` (absolute) and ``~=`` (fractional, default
49:    tol: Optional[float] = None
54:        if self.op not in _OPS:
56:                             f"expected one of {sorted(_OPS)}")

## Result

**`results/deepseek_ota/`** — `STATUS: SUCCESS`, `TAPEOUT_READY: True`

**Topology** (simplest that meets all specs post-layout): 6-MOS single-stage OTA — NMOS diff pair (XM1/XM2), PMOS current-mirror load (XM3 diode / XM4 mirror), NMOS tail (XM5) mirroring external `IBIAS` through diode-connected XM6, so IBIAS is a true 20 µA current input. Sized (`W_M3/M4=2.4u`, tail 2:1 mirror) so the output DC holds 1.65 V ± 0.5 V across the whole PVT grid.

**Post-layout (extracted) results** — all PASS:

| Metric | Extracted | Target |
|---|---|---|
| DC gain | 43.55 dB | ≥ 35 dB |
| GBW | 4.37 MHz | ≥ 1 MHz |
| Phase margin | 89.8° | ≥ 60° |
| Slew rise/fall | 6.5 / 6.8 V/µs | ≥ 0.5 |
| Supply current | 41.3 µA | ≤ 250 µA |
| Vout_DC | 1.744 V | 1.65 ± 0.5 V |
| Output swing | 0.0–3.3 V | 0.5–2.8 V |

Verification: Magic DRC clean (0), KLayout sign-off PASS, LVS "Circuits match uniquely" (6 devices / 8 nets), PEX extraction + PEX simulation PASS. Characterization: CMRR 79 dB, PSRR 43.6 dB, ICMR [0.8, 3.0] V, systematic offset −0.7 mV, input-referred noise 206 µV (1 Hz–100 MHz).

**PVT** (VDD 3.0/3.3/3.6 × −40/27/125 °C × typical/ff/ss): all 27 corners PASS; appended to the report as §13. Least-margined corner is ss/125 °C/3.0 V (gain 38.7 dB, Vout_DC 1.20 V).

**Deliverables**: `final_design_report.md` (with PVT addendum), `history.json`, `review_history.json`, `full_auto_result.json`, `final/` (ota.gds, Magic DRC report, LVS report, PEX netlist). Driver: `results/deepseek_ota/run_ota.py`.

Honest notes: the Devil reviewer logged one HIGH finding — `offset_mv` pre-vs-PEX relative shift of −235% — but that is −0.21 → −0.71 mV absolute (sub-millivolt, far inside any offset budget; non-blocking). The flow's gate treats PVT/Monte-Carlo as `NOT REQUIRED` by design, so the PVT run is a separate harness (same measurement code) rather than an in-flow gate.
